\displaystyle\leq\E_{A}[\frac{\delta(F^{2}|_{a})}{{\ell-|A|\choose i-|A|}}\E_{B_{2}}[f_{i}(a,b_{2})^{2}]]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{{\ell-|A|\choose i-|A|}}\E_{A}[\gamma\E_{B_{2}}[f_{i}(a,b_{2})^{2}]]+\E_{A}[(\delta(F^{2}|_{A})-\gamma)\left(\frac{\E_{B_{2}}[f_{i}(a,b_{2})^{2}]}{{\ell-|A|\choose i-|A|}}\right)],

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\gamma}{{\ell-|A|\choose i-|A|}}\cdot\frac{\eta_{i}}{{\ell\choose i}}+\E_{A}[(\delta(F^{2}|_{A})-\gamma)\left(\frac{\E_{B_{2}}[f_{i}(a,b_{2})^{2}]}{{\ell-|A|\choose i-|A|}}\right)],

where in the first inequality we have used Lemma
C.12
to bound
\E_{B_{1}}[f_{i}(a,b_{1})^{2}]
for each
a\sim A
and the last inequality uses Lemma
C.11
. Similarly we can bound the second term in equation (
50
):

\E_{C}[\E_{D_{1}}[f_{i}(c,d_{1})^{2}]\E_{D_{2}}[f_{i}(c,d_{2})^{2}]]\leq\frac{\gamma}{{\ell-|C|\choose i-|C|}}\cdot\frac{\eta_{i}}{{\ell\choose i}}+\E_{C}[(\delta(F^{2}|_{C})-\gamma)\left(\frac{\E_{D_{2}}[f_{i}(c,d_{2})^{2}]}{{\ell-|C|\choose i-|C|}}\right)].

Plugging in these bounds into equation (
50
) and setting
{\varepsilon}=\left(\frac{O(i)}{\ell}\right)^{\frac{|A|-|C|}{2}}
we get:

\displaystyle\E_{X}[f_{i}(I_{1})f_{i}(I_{2})f_{i}(I_{3})f_{i}(I_{4})]

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{O(i)}{\ell}\right)^{\frac{4i-|A|-|C|}{2}}\gamma\eta_{i}+\E_{A}[\left(\frac{O(i)}{\ell}\right)^{\frac{2i-|A|-|C|}{2}}\E_{B_{2}}[f_{i}(a,b_{2})^{2}]\cdot(\delta(F^{2}|_{a})-\gamma)]

\displaystyle+\E_{C}[\left(\frac{O(i)}{\ell}\right)^{\frac{2i-|A|-|C|}{2}}\E_{D_{2}}[f_{i}(c,d_{2})^{2}](\delta(F^{2}|_{c})-\gamma)].

We know that
4i-|A|-|C|=2d
and substituting this gives the lemma statement.

C.7
Combining the Upper and Lower Bounds on
\|F_{i}\|_{4}
: the Level
i
Inequality

Combining the upper and lower bounds on
\E[F_{i}(X)^{4}]
we can now prove an upper bound on
\eta_{i}
.

Lemma C.17

(Level
i
inequality)
.

There exist positive constants
c_{0},\ldots,c_{i}\leq\exp(i)
such that given the indeterminates
\{F(X)\}_{X\in V(C)}
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{inv}\,\,\vdash_{O(1)}\,\,\eta_{i}\leq\exp(i)\gamma^{1/3}\delta+B(F)+\sum_{j=0}^{i}\frac{c_{j}\ell^{i}}{\gamma}\E_{a\sim[n]^{j}}[\E_{b\sim[n]^{i-j}}[f_{i}(a,b)^{2}]\cdot(\delta(F^{2}|_{a})-\gamma)],

where
B(F)=\frac{4}{3}\E_{X}[(F-F^{3})F_{i}]+\exp(i)\gamma^{1/3}\E_{X}[F^{4}-F]
.

Proof.

Let
\delta=\E_{X}[F(X)]
and
\eta_{i}=\E_{X}[F_{i}^{2}(X)]
. Under the Booleanity axioms on
F
, we have proved an upper and lower bound on
{\E_{X}[F_{i}^{4}(X)]}
,

•

Upper Bound in Lemma
C.14
:

\E_{X}[F_{i}(X)^{4}]\leq\exp(i)\gamma\eta_{i}+\sum_{j=0}^{i}c^{\prime}_{j}\ell^{i}\E_{a\sim[n]^{j}}[\E_{b\sim[n]^{i-j}}[f_{i}(a,b)^{2}]\cdot(\delta(F^{2}|_{a})-\gamma)],

(51)

for some positive constants
c^{\prime}_{j}\leq\exp(i)
.

•

Lower Bound in Lemma
C.13
: For all
{\varepsilon}\in\mathbb{R}
we get,

\E_{X}[F_{i}^{4}(X)]\geq 4{\varepsilon}^{3}\eta_{i}-3{\varepsilon}^{4}\delta+B_{1}(F),

(52)

where
B_{1}(F)=4{\varepsilon}^{3}\E_{X}[(F^{3}-F)F_{i}]+3{\varepsilon}^{4}\E_{X}[F-F^{4}]
.

Combining (
51
) and (
52
),
setting
{\varepsilon}=(\exp(i)\gamma)^{1/3}
and dividing
by
3{\varepsilon}^{3}
yields

\displaystyle\eta_{i}\leq\exp(i)\gamma^{1/3}\delta+\sum_{j=0}^{i}\frac{c^{\prime}_{j}}{3\gamma\exp(i)}\ell^{i}\E_{a\sim[n]^{j}}[\E_{b\sim[n]^{i-j}}[f_{i}(a,b)^{2}]\cdot(\delta(F^{2}|_{a})-\gamma)]-\frac{B_{1}(F)}{3\gamma\exp(i)}.

The proof is thus completed by setting
c_{j}=\frac{c^{\prime}_{j}}{3\exp(i)}
and

B(F)=\frac{4}{3}\E_{X}[(F-F^{3})F_{i}]+\exp(i)\gamma^{1/3}\E_{X}[F^{4}-F]
.
∎

C.8
Expansion of Pseudorandom sets

Lemma
C.17
directly implies an expansion theorem for
C_{n,\ell,\alpha}
, as follows:

Theorem C.18

(Expansion Theorem for
C_{n,\ell,\alpha}
)
.

For all
\alpha\in(0,1)
, all integers
\ell\geq 1/\alpha
and
n\geq\ell
, the following holds: Let
C_{n,\ell,\alpha}
be the Johnson-approximating Cayley graph. For every constant
\gamma\in(0,1)
and positive integer
r\leq O(\ell)
, every permutation-invariant function
F:V(C)\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
that is
(r,\gamma)
-pseudorandom:

1.

\mathcal{A}_{inv}(F)\cup\{F(X)\in[0,1]\}_{X\in V(C)}\,\vdash_{O(1)}\,\\
\langle F,LF\rangle\geq\E[F](1-(1-\alpha)^{r+1})(1-\gamma^{1/3}\exp(r))-\sum_{j=0}^{r}\frac{c_{j}\ell^{j}}{\gamma}\E_{a\sim[n]^{j}}[q_{a}(F)({\delta(F^{2}|_{a})-\gamma)}]+B(F).

2.

\{F(X)\in[0,1]\}\,\,\,\vdash_{2}\,\,\,0\leq q_{a}(F)\leq 1,

where for all
j\leq r
,
c_{j}
’s are positive constants
\leq\exp(r)
, for all
a\in[n]^{j},j\leq r
,
q_{a}(F)
are degree
2
polynomials and
B(F)=\frac{4}{3}\E_{X}[(F^{3}-F)\sum_{i=0}^{r}F_{i}]+c\E_{X}[F-F^{4}]
, for a positive constant
c\leq\exp(r)\gamma^{1/3}
.

Proof.

We first have that:
\langle F,LF\rangle=\E[F^{2}]-\langle F,CF\rangle
, where
L
and
C
denote the normalized Laplacian and adjacency matrix of the graph
C_{k,\ell,\alpha}
respectively. Let
1=\lambda_{1}\geq\ldots\lambda_{\ell}\geq-1
denote the eigenvalues of
C
. We will henceforth bound
\langle F,CF\rangle
:

\displaystyle\langle F,CF\rangle=\sum_{i\in[l]}\lambda_{i}\eta_{i}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=0}^{r}\lambda_{i}\eta_{i}+(\E[F^{2}]-\sum_{i=1}^{r}\eta_{i})\lambda_{r+1}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\lambda_{r+1})\sum_{i=0}^{r}\eta_{i}+\E[F]\lambda_{r+1},

where in the last step we use that
F(X)\in[0,1]
. Let
\delta
denote
\E[F]
. Using Lemma
C.17
we get:

\displaystyle\langle F,CF\rangle

\displaystyle\leq(1-\lambda_{r+1})\sum_{i=0}^{r}\left(\exp(i)\gamma^{1/3}\delta+B_{i}(F)+\sum_{j=0}^{i}\frac{c^{\prime}_{ij}\ell^{i}}{\gamma}\E_{a\sim[n]^{j}}[\E_{b\sim[n]^{i-j}}[f_{i}(a,b)^{2}]\cdot(\delta(F^{2}|_{a})-\gamma)]\right)+\delta\lambda_{r+1}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\lambda_{r+1})(\exp(r)\gamma^{1/3}\delta)+(1-\lambda_{r+1})\sum_{i}B_{i}(F)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{r}\E_{a\sim[n]^{j}}[(\delta(F^{2}|_{a})-\gamma)(\sum_{i=j}^{r}(1-\lambda_{r+1})\frac{c^{\prime}_{ij}\ell^{i}}{\gamma}\E_{b\sim[n]^{i-j}}[f_{i}(a,b)^{2}])]+\delta\lambda_{r+1},

for some positive constants
c^{\prime}_{ij}\leq\exp(i)
and
B_{i}(F)=\frac{4}{3}\E_{X}[(F-F^{3})F_{i}]+\exp(i)\gamma^{1/3}\E_{X}[F^{4}-F]
.

Let us now look at the multipliers of
\delta(F^{2}|_{a})-\gamma
, for each
a\in[n]^{j}
consider the polynomial:
q_{a}(F)=\frac{1}{c_{j}\ell^{j}}\sum_{i=j}^{r}(1-\lambda_{r+1})c^{\prime}_{ij}\ell^{i}\E_{b\sim[n]^{i-j}}[f_{i}(a,b)^{2}]
, for fixed constants
c_{j}\leq\exp(r)
to be determined later. Using Lemma
C.12
to bound
\E_{b\sim[n]^{i-j}}[f_{i}(a,b)^{2}]
we get that:

\displaystyle\{F(X)\in[0,1]\}\vdash_{2}~~~~0\leq q_{a}(F)\leq\frac{1}{c_{j}}\sum_{i=j}^{r}(1-\lambda_{r+1})c^{\prime}_{ij}\ell^{i-j}\frac{\delta(F^{2}|_{a})}{{\ell-j\choose i-j}}\leq 1,

for
c_{j}=\sum_{i=j}^{r}(1-\lambda_{r+1})c^{\prime}_{ij}\frac{\ell^{i-j}}{{\ell-j\choose i-j}}\leq\exp(r)
. Plugging in the polynomials
q_{a}(F)
and rearranging yields

\langle F,LF\rangle\geq\delta(1-\lambda_{r+1})(1-\gamma^{1/3}\exp(r))-\sum_{j=0}^{r}\frac{c_{j}\ell^{j}}{\gamma}\E_{a\sim[n]^{j}}[q_{a}(F)(\delta(F^{2}|_{a})-\gamma)]+B(F),

for some positive constants
c_{j}\leq\exp(r)
and
B(F)=\frac{4}{3}\E_{X}[(F^{3}-F)\sum_{i=0}^{r}F_{i}]+c\E_{X}[F-F^{4}]
, for a positive constant
c\leq\exp(r)\gamma^{1/3}
.

We will now plug in upper bounds for
\lambda_{i}
. From Lemma
C.4
, we have that
\lambda_{i}=\frac{\binom{\ell-|T|}{(1-\alpha)\ell-|T|}}{\binom{\ell}{(1-\alpha)\ell}}
for
i\leq(1-\alpha)\ell
and
0
otherwise. One can check that
{\lambda_{i}\leq(1-\alpha)\lambda_{i-1}}
for all
i
between
1
and
\ell
. Since
\lambda_{0}=1
, we get that
\lambda_{i}\leq(1-\alpha)^{i}
, thus completing the proof of the lemma.

∎
